# Evidence and certificate audit — 2026-09-01

This audit treats `disc/world_map.bin` as the retail world-overlay authority,
loaded at guest address `0x8006FAF0`. Findings are recorded without repair
proposals. The repository scope is pinned to the audit-start commit
`2439d53d42dc926814daece415adc99516520c64`; commits arriving in the shared
worktree after the audit began are outside this report. At that revision,
exactly 32 tracked `pc_port/src/world_map_*.c` files begin with the bracketed
`Exact native transcription of retail [...]` claim used by Pass 2. Reproduce
that scope with `git ls-tree -r --name-only 2439d53d -- pc_port/src` and inspect
each first line with `git show 2439d53d:<path>`.

The shared checkout advanced beyond the pin during the audit; it had reached
`6c68006f3d9bbca494976ae6e85d2ddbe9d25734` when the plain-line references
below were recorded and continued advancing afterward. Later-added evidence/TUs
are excluded. Twenty-three Pass-4 rows and the Pass-5 per-file convenience rows
use plain `nl`/`rg` commands to locate statements that were unchanged at that
checkpoint; scope-sensitive findings and all census totals use explicit
`git show`/`git grep` commands against the pin. The retail
blob is a gitignored local input, not an object available from the pinned tree;
that chain-of-custody limitation is recorded in Pass 1.

Pass 1 rows use this shell function:

```sh
anchor() {
  local start=$1 end=$2
  dd if=disc/world_map.bin bs=1 \
    skip=$((start - 0x8006FAF0)) count=$((end - start)) status=none |
    sha256sum
}
```

Pass-1 findings:

- All 151 stated interval hashes recompute exactly from the supplied local
  `disc/world_map.bin`. No address-range anchor is mismatched or fabricated
  relative to that byte image.
- That agreement is an internal-consistency result, not independent retail-image
  authentication. Pinned `.gitignore:3` excludes `disc/*`, the pinned tree holds
  only `disc/.gitkeep`, and `world_map.bin` cannot be recovered with `git show
  2439d53d:disc/world_map.bin`. The local file's whole-image SHA-256 is
  `4c15fd32b3a03d7cd5ea4403dcaabc70abaf99b6aaca65d63d6866803edaac70`,
  exactly the value repeated by 14 pinned evidence READMEs. This proves that the
  interval claims, whole-image claims, disassembly, and port loader consistently
  refer to the same supplied blob; the repository alone does not supply an
  independent extraction manifest or versioned byte source that proves the
  blob's retail provenance. Reproduce with `git show 2439d53d:.gitignore | nl
  -ba | sed -n '1,5p'; git ls-tree -r --name-only 2439d53d -- disc;
  sha256sum disc/world_map.bin; git grep -l
  4c15fd32b3a03d7cd5ea4403dcaabc70abaf99b6aaca65d63d6866803edaac70
  2439d53d -- 'docs/evidence/**/README.md' | wc -l`.
- The port-loaded-image widening agrees **by construction, not as an
  independent authority**. At the pin, `world_map_init.c:2300-2337` sets
  `dst = PSX_ADDR(0x8006FAF0)` and performs one untransformed
  `fread(dst, 1, 180422, fopen("disc/world_map.bin"))`; therefore every loaded
  interval is byte-identical to the same file used above unless the read is
  short, which the function rejects. Reproduce with `git show
  2439d53d:pc_port/src/world_map_init.c | nl -ba | sed -n '2300,2338p'` and
  `stat -c '%s' disc/world_map.bin` (180422 bytes).
- **SUSPECT (wrong length):** the README corpus contains 25 ellipsized hash
  references on 18 lines: 24 expose eight hex digits and one exposes 16,
  representing 17 distinct prefixes.
  They occur at `w34c11-cd-loader:74,93`,
  `w34c12-provenance-closure:33,34,44`, `w34c2-static-data-load:92,94`,
  `w34c3-tile-slot-population:37,50,51`, `w34c5-tile-producers:66,67`,
  `w34c6-height-byte:44`, `w34c7-triangle-order:44,45`,
  `w34c9-entry-placement:47`, `w34n107-mode16-distortion-strip:95`, and
  `w34n108-mode16-width-control:105` (all paths are
  `docs/evidence/<name>/README.md`). The distinct prefixes are `0064a78c`,
  `1664fa97`, `26e59e42`, `2a5aaa0a`, `38c57657`, `6daf6cb0`,
  `6f51c91b`, `850ce0f1`, `9413ab2b`, `abf1a963`, `adacdb46`, `ae06268a`,
  `de1764e1`, `eb6195d9`, `ec2ea281`, `ec2ea281009bfff1`, and `f73c628e`.
  Their explicit ellipses
  show that they are abbreviated capture/baseline references rather than
  asserted complete digest values, and none is an address-range anchor in the
  table. The pair `ec2ea281…`/`ec2ea281009bfff1…` is also **SUSPECT under the
  shared-prefix rule**: the READMEs identify both as the W34C6 capture, and
  `scratchpad/w34c6_height_byte.foi5kq/captures.sha256` expands the latter to
  `ec2ea281009bfff1d9bb7cf6c7bbbec4f94e45974c2b816dbc10244f95aa38e9`,
  so the available local witness indicates inconsistent abbreviation of one
  digest rather than two distinct digests with a fabricated affix. The
  scratchpad manifest is untracked and therefore corroborative, not a pinned
  evidence anchor. Reproduce the exhaustive README occurrence inventory with `git grep -P -n -o
  '(?<![0-9a-fA-F])[0-9a-fA-F]{8,63}(?:…|\.\.\.)' 2439d53d --
  'docs/evidence/**/README.md'`. The other short hexadecimal tokens on lines
  mentioning hashes are labeled commit/build IDs (`3e10093a`) or a PCSX BIOS
  identifier (`8d8cb7e4`), not complete SHA-256 claims.
- The pinned README corpus has 414 full-length hash occurrences representing 252
  distinct values. No two distinct 64-hex-digit hashes share a prefix or suffix
  of eight or more hexadecimal digits. Exact duplicates are repeated
  whole-image or accepted-frame anchors, not distinct values with a suspicious
  shared affix. Reproduce the corpus with `git grep -P -o
  '(?<![0-9a-fA-F])[0-9a-fA-F]{64}(?![0-9a-fA-F])' 2439d53d --
  'docs/evidence/**/README.md'`; strip the `commit:path:` prefix, then group the
  unique results by `substr($0,1,8)` and `substr($0,57,8)`.

## Pass 2 — constant derivation

Complete. The whole-file scope is the 32 shipped translation units whose
first line says `Exact native transcription of retail [...]`; mixed-purpose
`world_map_common_tail.c` is not counted as a whole-file exact transcription;
its isolated exact leaf is included in the completed helper-TU widening below.

After removing comments, strings, identifiers, type widths, compiled-out
mutant arms, and duplicate occurrences, these 32 units contain 1,297 distinct
`(file, numeric literal)` entries. (An earlier interim count incorrectly put a
token boundary before the C integer suffix, thereby omitting literals ending in
`u`; that count was discarded. A later hand-carried total of 1,302 was also
discarded: a fresh compiler-token recount of all 32 source blobs at the pinned
commit yields 1,297, exactly the number of ledger rows.) Standard C integer
suffixes are removed for
grouping, while source radix and spelling are retained (`4u` groups with `4`,
but `0x04u` is a separate token). Mechanical value matching is used only to
locate candidates. A row becomes `DERIVED` only after its operation, sign,
shift, and fixed-point scale agree with the cited instruction.

The production-path table is complete. Repeated uses of the
same token in one file are grouped into one row, with every source line listed.

The bounded helper-TU widening is complete. It covers every shipped helper TU
that explicitly claims an exact retail transcription, the isolated exact leaf
in the mixed-purpose common-tail TU, and the adjacent `0x8009623C`-`0x80096668`
queue chain selected after its certificate contradiction surfaced. The
explicitly exact `world_map_helper_71fec.c` is complete: all 11 active numeric tokens are
derived from retail `[0x80071FEC,0x80072090)`. The explicitly exact
`world_map_helper_86124.c` is also complete across its three paired-free leaves:
six guest-global literals are derived, while three pointer-compatibility guard
literals are **UNMATCHED**. The retail leaves load the globals and call
`HeapFree` directly; none contains the port's zero/KSEG/range test.
The widening additionally covers queue-record helper
`world_map_helper_96328.c`: all ten active `(file, literal)` entries reconcile
to retail `[0x80096328,0x800963E4)`. Mutant-only literals are excluded by the
same production-preprocessor rule as the exact-TU corpus.
The mixed-purpose common-tail TU is not treated wholesale, but its small exact
leaf `wm_800894C8` is now isolated: its sole production-semantic numeric literal
in the C body reconciles to retail `[0x800894C8,0x80089514)`. Test-observer PC
arguments compile away in the ordinary build and are not counted; values named
only through header macros are not misattributed as literals of this C file.
In `world_map_helper_9623c.c`, the exact `wm_800963E4` sort leaf's four active
literal groups are derived. The immediately following `wm_800964B0` body is a
larger contradiction: all three of its literal groups are **UNMATCHED** because
the C implements a two-argument 40-byte self/copy loop, while retail
`[0x800964B0,0x800965A4)` is a one-argument bubble sort over 16-byte records.
The live port caller `wm_800965A4` passes the same record as both C arguments,
so its call is a no-op copy rather than the retail file-backend sort.
The same file-backend chain is already broken at `wm_800962B0`: C computes
`(index*3-index)<<7` (256-byte block stride), while retail computes
`((index*3)*4-index)<<7` (1,408-byte block stride). Its `3` and `7` tokens occur
in retail, but are classified **UNMATCHED** because the scaling operation they
participate in is not the retail operation.
The publisher `world_map_helper_965a4.c` adds a third defect: its nested test
succeeds when the first record is zero, while retail `beqz` at `0x800965E4`
goes directly to the failure/clear return. Its remaining numeric spellings are
derived, but the zero comparison is **UNMATCHED**; it then reaches the wrong
two-argument sort contract described above.
The deterministic index-1 consequence is that the writer places records at
`base+256`, while the publisher examines and publishes `base+1408`, 1,152 bytes
later. Because the publisher wrongly accepts the zero first record, the two
defects compose, when that untouched target word is zero, into a successful
publication of the wrong block rather than a failure.
At this completed widening frontier the ledger contains 1,349 data rows: 1,340
`DERIVED` and nine `UNMATCHED`. The 32 required exact TUs remain 1,297/1,297
derived; every unmatched row is in the helper widening.
In particular, the host masks
`0x80000000` and
`0xFFF00000` are not retail immediates: together they implement the sign fill
of retail's arithmetic `sra 12`, so their witnesses are the shift instructions
rather than coincidental constants elsewhere.

The `0x800964B0` mismatch is also a certificate-strength finding. W34N48 says
its production-linked certificate proves the backend-specific terminator/flush
pair, but `w34n48_98cc0_prod_test.c:127-149` replaces all four queue/publish
functions—including `wm_800965A4`—with event-recording stubs. It therefore
proves that `wm_80098CC0` selects the file-backend symbol, not that the shipped
publisher or sorter has retail behavior. W34C11 behaviorally covers only the
12-byte CD queue/sort path (`9623C -> 96328 -> 963E4`); its not-ready PC-file
consumer is also a stub. The W34N48 natural gate recorded zero calls. Thus both
certificates pass while the shipped file-backend writer addresses the wrong
block, the publisher accepts empty blocks, and its flush calls a semantically
wrong body. Reproduce with `git show
2439d53d:pc_port/tests/w34n48_98cc0_prod_test.c | nl -ba | sed -n
'127,150p;320,354p'`, `git show
2439d53d:pc_port/tests/run_w34n48_98cc0.sh | nl -ba | sed -n '6,24p'`, `git show
2439d53d:pc_port/tests/w34c11_cd_loader_prod_test.c | nl -ba | sed -n
'77,108p'`, `git show
2439d53d:docs/evidence/w34n48-paged-terrain-refill-98cc0/README.md | nl -ba |
sed -n '60,92p'`, and `git grep -n wm_800964B0 2439d53d -- pc_port/src
pc_port/tests`.

An isolated runtime replay of the pinned tree does not contradict that result:
`run_w34n48_98cc0.sh` exits zero with `W34N48 0x80098CC0 FULL CERTIFICATE PASS;
M1-M10 DETECTED`, and `run_w34c11_cd_loader_prod_test.sh` exits zero after all
three focused production regimes and reports `M1` through `M9` detected. Those
passes establish that the checked-in harnesses and their mutants behave as the
harnesses specify; the source/linkage evidence above establishes that the
specification does not execute the three shipped file-backend bodies whose
retail mismatches are at issue. Reproduce without using the moving checkout
with `audit_dir=$(mktemp -d); mkdir "$audit_dir/tree"; git archive 2439d53d |
tar -x -C "$audit_dir/tree"; (cd "$audit_dir/tree" && bash
pc_port/tests/run_w34n48_98cc0.sh && bash
pc_port/tests/run_w34c11_cd_loader_prod_test.sh)`.

Pinned blame traces the wrong writer and sorter unchanged to `369b1353`, and
the publisher to `6c81b111`. W34C11 (`41e65bc9`) later edits the combined
`world_map_helper_9623c.c`, but replaces only `wm_800963E4`; its direct queue
test is correspondingly limited to the 12-byte CD family. W34N48's runner then
compiles only `world_map_helper_98cc0.c` beside its stub-bearing test. Reproduce
the provenance with `git blame 2439d53d -L 39,55 --
pc_port/src/world_map_helper_9623c.c`, `git blame 2439d53d -L 98,105 --
pc_port/src/world_map_helper_9623c.c`, `git blame 2439d53d -L 20,45 --
pc_port/src/world_map_helper_965a4.c`, and `git show --name-only 41e65bc9`.

There are correct, hash-gated implementations and focused mutant certificates
for all three broken helpers in side commits: `wm_800962B0` in `4cba9ce4`
identifies the `0x580` block stride, the same commit's `wm_800965A4` rejects
empty blocks, and `wm_800964B0` in `8f8ac163` identifies the same one-argument
16-byte-key sort found above. Their stated slice hashes
`ecc6b8ff0a942d007e4b5eaa9c89ec3834d62960d9c1736425f4a2006785725c` and
`6393023f3f0436ba14cc3178433ed15c867bbcebf33540483856ee95ae964ab7`, plus
`125fa0364f7974f4a59d5701be4e21fbd671565404ade24bbc4a27857eb28ca6`,
all recompute from `disc/world_map.bin`; they are in `REPORT.md` files, so they
are not among Pass 1's required README anchors. Neither side commit is an
ancestor of the pinned audit commit, and none of the three focused source/test
pairs exists in the pinned tree; `build_port.sh` instead compiles the combined
`world_map_helper_9623c.c`.
The absent focused reports record 7/7 mutants for `962B0`, 4/4 for `964B0`,
and 8/8 for `965A4`, including wrong-block, skip-sort, and skip-empty mutants
that directly correspond to the pinned defects.
Reproduce with
`git merge-base --is-ancestor 4cba9ce4 2439d53d; echo $?` and `git merge-base
--is-ancestor 8f8ac163 2439d53d; echo $?` (both result `1`), `git show
4cba9ce4:docs/evidence/w34b24-i33-962b0/REPORT.md`, `git show
4cba9ce4:docs/evidence/w34b24-i37-965a4/REPORT.md`, `git show
8f8ac163:docs/evidence/w34b24-i35-964b0/REPORT.md`, and `git show
2439d53d:pc_port/build_port.sh | rg '9623c|964b0'`; recompute the side-report
anchors with `dd if=disc/world_map.bin bs=1 skip=$((0x267c0)) count=120
status=none | sha256sum`, `dd if=disc/world_map.bin bs=1 skip=$((0x26ab4))
count=196 status=none | sha256sum`, and `dd if=disc/world_map.bin bs=1
skip=$((0x269c0)) count=244 status=none | sha256sum`.

## Pass 3 — test expectation churn

Complete. The pinned history contains 206 commits touching 338 matching test
paths. A zero-context modified-hunk scan found 98 candidate hunks. Every
candidate was inspected; additions of entirely new tests/cases, type-suffix-only
edits, diagnostic text, assertion-label renames, fixture plumbing, oracle
implementation rewrites that retain the old expected results, and mutant-source
matching edits are excluded. The semantic changes below are grouped where one
commit makes the same expectation correction at several fixture records.

| Commit | File/line | Old expectation | New expectation | Retail justification | Reproduction command |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|

The `NO DIRECT RETAIL CITATION`/`MESSAGE ONLY` labels describe the evidence
shipped with each expectation-changing commit, not necessarily the truth of
the new value. A post-hoc retail check independently confirms two of the four:
at `0x80071488` retail calls the second scheduler and `0x80071490` is the next
instruction, supporting `01dc22ca`; and the loop at `0x80097C1C` initializes
255, stores at `C580`, decrements the counter and address by one/four, and
branches while nonnegative, yielding exactly 256 stores through `C184` and
supporting `d4e1c5cc`. No equally direct retail witness was established in this
audit for `eaffa2c2`'s composite scheduler state/count oracle or `d929314b`'s
exact terrain delta fixtures. Reproduce the two confirmations with
`mips-linux-gnu-objdump -D -b binary -m mips:3000 -EL
--adjust-vma=0x8006faf0 --start-address=0x80071460
--stop-address=0x800714B0 disc/world_map.bin` and the same command with
`--start-address=0x80097BC0 --stop-address=0x80097C50`.

The history/message scan found one explicit diagnostic-failure sequence tied to
an expectation change: `60cc7fc2` says a three-frame run exposed the raw-native
image queue representation, then changes the image-transfer certificate from
7/7 to 8/8. It is not flagged because the same commit's retail evidence document
states the domain rule and the new behavioral case. No expectation change after
a failing run without retail justification was established from the commit
messages, bodies, diffs, or same-commit evidence documents. Reproduce the scan
with `git log 2439d53d42dc926814daece415adc99516520c64 --format='%H%x09%s%n%b' -- 'pc_port/tests/*_prod_test.c' 'pc_port/tests/run_*.sh' | rg -ni -C2 'fail(ed|ing)|actual[ :=]|after.*fail'`.

## Pass 4 — pointer-domain sweep

Complete for all shipped `pc_port/src/world_map_*.c`, then widened across the
remaining 20 pinned `pc_port/src/*.{c,h}` files. The inventory treats a retail
RAM word/record member subsequently consumed as an address as a guest-address
field. Branch alternatives and loop instances are grouped, but every static
world-map store site is represented. The widened rows retain candidate stores
that prove to be GPU encodings or native-only object fields so that their
exclusion is reproducible rather than implicit. Native authorities
(`D_8005945C`, `D_8006259C`, SpriteData links, and slot `+0x4C`) were checked
against their located consumers. The prior W34C15 port-owned-source sweep was
also rechecked at the audit commit; its three native-stack/guest-helper
crossings are guest-frame adaptations there and are calls, not field stores.

The material Pass-4 result remains a mis-certified exercised guest-field store:
W34N5 passes specifically because it asserts that a raw host pointer belongs in
retail guest word `0x8006258C`. The natural route's only witness returned NULL,
so it did not exercise the nonzero domain crossing. The widening found no
second non-world guest-field defect at the pinned commit, but it did expose
untested production writers in native 32-bit pointer graphs whose classification
depends on the low-address invariant. The newly traced file-queue path is
guest-domain-correct at its located callers, but its writer/publisher bodies are
untested and semantically wrong for non-domain reasons recorded in Pass 2.
An isolated replay of the pinned W34N5 source and test exits zero in O0, O2,
and nonrecovering-UBSan regimes and reports all M1-M3 detected. This confirms
that the certificate actually accepts the synthetic nonzero raw value described
in the table; it does not rehabilitate that value's domain. The archive needs
the checkout's ignored PsyCross headers supplied through `CPATH`, because that
external dependency is not present in `git archive`. Reproduce with
`checkout=$PWD; audit_dir=$(mktemp -d); mkdir "$audit_dir/tree"; git archive 2439d53d | tar
-x -C "$audit_dir/tree"; (cd "$audit_dir/tree" &&
CPATH="$checkout/pc_port/extern/PsyCross/include:$checkout/pc_port/extern/PsyCross/include/psx"
bash pc_port/tests/run_w34n5_wds_lifecycle.sh)`.
No pinned port read of guest word `0x8006258C` was located outside the
certificate: the immediately following consumer explicitly says it does not
read that word, while native teardown/users read the separate
`g_GameCurLoadedWDS` authority. Accordingly, this audit establishes a guest-word
representation defect and a certificate that blesses it, but not a pinned
runtime dereference or crash caused by that word. Reproduce with `git grep -n
-E '6258C|g_GameCurLoadedWDS|WM_CONSUMER_RESULT' 2439d53d -- pc_port/src
pc_port/tests src/field`.
`D_801E8670` is not a store candidate at
this commit: `func_801E742C` is still an unported producer, the table stays NULL,
and its raw-cast consumer is unreachable. Reproduce that pinned exclusion with
`git show 2439d53d:src/field/main/main.c | nl -ba | sed -n '225,248p'` and the full
candidate sweep with `git grep -n -E
'PsxMemory_GuestAddr|host_ptr_to_psx_u32|wm_host_to_psx|\(u32\)\(uintptr_t\)|0x80086700|RECPTR|PcPort_AddPrimDomainAware|HeapAlloc\('
2439d53d -- pc_port/src` and the pinned scope count with `git ls-tree -r
--name-only 2439d53d pc_port/src | rg '^pc_port/src/.*\.(c|h)$' | rg -v
'/world_map_' | wc -l`.

## Pass 5 — suppression and instrumentation inventory

Complete. The pinned shipped world-map TUs contain **184** exact `(void)name;`
statements in 58 files and **1,265 mutant-symbol occurrences in preprocessor
conditionals, macro definitions, and selected bodies** across 143 files (1,260
source lines). Of those, 1,256 occurrences on 1,251 lines are direct
`#if`/`#elif`-family directives, one occurrence is a continued `defined(...)`
condition, four are macro definitions, and four are body uses. The exhaustive
per-file rows below preserve every line/symbol occurrence. `UNPROVEN` means no
retail instruction using that discarded C value was established; it does not
mean the value is proven dead. This conservative label includes test-only `pc`
trace parameters, genuinely dead callback arguments, and values suppressed only
inside mutant branches. Six world-map TUs added after the pin account for 17
additional suppressions and 54 additional mutant-symbol occurrences in the
moving checkout; they are excluded from the counts and rows below.

The Pass-5 appendix reconciles exactly to the pin: its 58 discarded-value rows
contain 184 `(void)` statements, and its 143 mutant rows contain 1,265 symbol
occurrences on 1,260 distinct source lines. Independent `git grep` censuses
produce the same four totals. The five pass tables contain respectively 151,
1,349, 16, 36, and 216 data rows. Reproduce the pinned census with `git grep
-P -o '\(void\)\s*[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*\s*;' 2439d53d --
'pc_port/src/world_map_*.c'` and `git grep -o -E
'[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*MUTANT[A-Za-z0-9_]*' 2439d53d --
'pc_port/src/world_map_*.c'`; count unique `commit:path` prefixes for files and
unique `commit:path:line` prefixes for mutant-symbol source lines.


## Top findings by severity

1. **HIGH — W34N5 certifies a host-domain value in a guest-address field.**
   `world_map_init.c:6761` writes raw `SoundLoadWdsFile` return bits to retail
   word `0x8006258C`. `w34n5_wds_lifecycle_prod_test.c:78-81` returns synthetic
   nonzero host pointer `0x00123450` and requires the guest word to equal those
   raw bits. The accepted natural-route witness reached the writer only with a
   NULL return, so the live nonzero crossing remains masked. No pinned port
   consumer of the guest word was located—the native twin is used instead—so
   the established defect is representational/mis-certification, not a proved
   downstream crash. Reproduce with
   `git show 2439d53d:pc_port/src/world_map_init.c | nl -ba | sed -n
   '6752,6765p'; git show
   2439d53d:pc_port/tests/w34n5_wds_lifecycle_prod_test.c | sed -n
   '65,90p'`.

2. **HIGH — the file-backend queue certificate substitutes stubs over three
   wrong shipped helpers.** `world_map_helper_9623c.c:47` uses a 256-byte block
   stride where retail uses 1,408 bytes. The same file's lines 99-105 implement
   `wm_800964B0` as a two-argument 40-byte copy loop, but retail
   `[0x800964B0,0x800965A4)` is a one-argument bubble sort over 16-byte records.
   `world_map_helper_965a4.c:30-38` also publishes empty blocks and self-copies,
   so it performs no sort. W34N48 claims backend-specific terminator/flush
   coverage but stubs `wm_800965A4`; W34C11 covers the separate 12-byte CD
   sorter and stubs the PC-file route; the natural W34N48 gate was dormant.
   Correct focused implementations exist in side commits `4cba9ce4` and
   `8f8ac163`, but neither is an ancestor and all three focused TU/test pairs
   are absent from the pinned build; all three stated retail slice hashes
   independently match
   the disc. The Pass-2 rows
   carry pinned source, disassembly, and test commands.

3. **HIGH — four expectation-change groups lack direct retail support in their
   commits.** `01dc22ca` changes cut PC/call count, `eaffa2c2` changes scheduler
   entry/callback/state expectations, `d4e1c5cc` changes a 0x400-byte clear
   extent, and `d929314b` replaces continuity/no-crash checks with exact terrain
   deltas/oracles. None changes an evidence README or cites a retail instruction
   in the changed assertion; `d4e1c5cc` has message-only justification. The
   post-hoc audit directly confirms the new `01dc22ca` cut/call and
   `d4e1c5cc` clear extent from disassembly, but did not establish an equally
   direct retail witness for the other two composite oracles. The Pass-3 table
   carries each exact diff command. No failing-run sequence without retail
   justification was established from history.

4. **MEDIUM — source spelling is used as semantic evidence in 14 assertion
   families, plus one mutant-construction family.** The most consequential is
   `run_w34n112_mode17_particles.sh:25-31`: it accepts the two conversion call
   strings without proving the converter's domain, reachability, overwrite
   behavior, or the consumer's observed value. Lifecycle case/call, frame-order,
   build-membership, and declaration-spelling checks have the same structural
   limitation. The fifteenth row source-matches mutations but then runs
   behavioral executables, so only its mutation construction—not its final
   verdict—is text-dependent. Reproduce all identified candidates with `git grep -n -E
   '(rg|grep|sed).*(pc_port/src|build_port\.sh|\$PROD|\$CALLER|\$HELPER|\$DRIVER)'
   2439d53d -- 'pc_port/tests/run_*.sh'`.

5. **MEDIUM — mutant implementations remain throughout shipped production
   TUs.** There are 1,265 mutant-symbol occurrences on 1,260 lines across 143
   `world_map_*.c` files; 1,257 occurrences belong to direct or continued
   preprocessor conditions and eight are macro-definition/body-use sites. The
   Pass-5 appendix lists every file, line,
   and symbol. Reproduce with `git grep -n -o -E
   '[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*MUTANT[A-Za-z0-9_]*' 2439d53d --
   'pc_port/src/world_map_*.c'`.

6. **MEDIUM — 184 named values are explicitly discarded without a proved retail
   use/deadness mapping.** They occur in 58 shipped world-map files. Many are
   mutant-only suppressions or test trace-PC parameters, but the exhaustive
   audit could not attach an exact retail-use instruction to any of these
   statements, so every row remains `UNPROVEN`. Reproduce with `git grep -n -o
   -P '\(void\)\s*[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*\s*;' 2439d53d --
   'pc_port/src/world_map_*.c'`.

7. **LOW — the retail byte authority is internally consistent but not
   repository-pinned.** `disc/world_map.bin` is excluded by `.gitignore` and is
   absent from the audit commit. Its current full hash matches the value in 14
   pinned READMEs, and all 151 interval hashes match it, but those checks all
   terminate at the same supplied local file. The repository alone cannot
   independently reproduce its extraction or authenticate its retail
   provenance. This prevents confirmation of a fabricated anchor, but also
   prevents the matching hashes from fully disproving coordinated fabrication.

8. **LOW — three additional helper literals are port-only compatibility
   constants rather than retail-derived.** In
   `world_map_helper_86124.c`, `0`, `0x80000000`, and `0x80200000` implement a
   pointer-domain compatibility guard absent from all three retail leaf ranges.
   Together with six unmatched literals in the wrong file-backend
   writer/publisher/sort bodies, the widened ledger has nine `UNMATCHED` rows.
   The other 1,340 audited literal rows are `DERIVED`; all 1,297 literals in
   the 32 exact-transcription TUs reconcile to retail instructions.

9. **INFORMATIONAL — no individual retail range hash was shown to be
   fabricated.** All 151 README range/SHA-256 anchors recompute exactly from
   the supplied `disc/world_map.bin`; subject to Finding 7's provenance limit,
   no mismatch or synthetic value was found. The port-loaded-image recheck
   agrees by construction because the port directly reads that same file
   without transformation.
   Twenty-five explicitly ellipsized capture/baseline references on 18 lines
   (24 with
   eight exposed hex digits, one with 16) are marked suspect under
   the requested wrong-length rule, but none is a retail range anchor. No
   suspicious shared prefix/suffix was found among distinct full-length
   hashes. The three side-branch REPORT hashes used to corroborate Finding 2
   also recompute exactly, although they were outside the required README
   corpus.

10. **INFORMATIONAL — several non-world native pointer writers lack direct
   production-body tests.** The AnimTask/SpriteData/work-list publications,
   deferred-image-node writer, and in-place model relocator store native
   pointers in 32-bit fields. Located consumers confirm these are native object
   graphs rather than guest-address fields, so they are not additional domain
   defects at the pinned commit; their correctness depends on the below-4-GiB,
   non-PIE invariant. No pinned test directly names `func_800233A4`,
   `func_8001D3F4`, `func_80025180`, or `func_8002C59C`. The widened Pass-4 rows
   carry the exact absence and source commands.
